an agreed framework, to ensure that there are no undue exposures to significant       在 協 定 之 框 架 內 管 理 其 利 率 風 險，以 確 保 不

uncertainty, market volatility, and geopolitical tensions are expected to continue     整及復甦。因此，經濟不確定性、市場波動性

                                                                                       二六年持續影響全球經濟狀況。

pace, reflecting fiscal constraints and prioritization of essential projects.          映出財政限制以及必要項目的優先順序。

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE REPORT
企業管治報告

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                                         企業管治常規

The Company and the Board are devoted to achieving and maintaining high                本公司及董事會致力達致及維持最高水平之
standard of corporate governance as the Board believes that effective corporate        企業管治，董事會相信，行之有效的企業管治
governance practices are fundamental in enhancing the shareholder value                常規對提升股東價值以及保障股東及其他權
and safeguarding the interests of the Shareholders and other stakeholders.             益相關者之利益至關重要。因此，本公司已採
Accordingly, the Company has adopted sound corporate governance principles             納 健 全 之 企 業 管 治 原 則，當 中 著 重 優 秀 之 董
that emphasise a quality Board, effective internal control, stringent disclosure       事會、有效之內部監控、嚴謹之披露常規以及
practices and transparency and accountability to all Shareholders.                     對所有股東之透明度及問責性。

The Company has complied with the code provisions set out in the Corporate             本 公 司 已 遵 守 上 市 規 則 附 錄C1所 載 之 企 業 管
Governance Code (the “CG Code”) contained in Appendix C1 to the Listing                治 守 則（「企 業 管 治 守 則」）之 守 則 條 文，惟 偏
Rules, except for the deviation from the code provision C.2.1.                         離守則條文第C.2.1條除外。

Under code provision C.2.1, the roles of chairman and chief executive should           根據守則條文第C.2.1條，主席與行政總裁之角
be separate and should not be performed by the same individual in order to             色應有區分，並不應由同一名人士兼任，以確
ensure that there is clear division of responsibilities between the chairman of the    保董事會主席與本公司行政總裁之職責有明
Board and the chief executive of the Company. The Company has not appointed            確區分。本公司並無委任主席，而董事會則領
a chairman and the Board provides leadership for the Company. Having                   導 本 公 司。考 慮 到 本 集 團 於 重 大 時 刻 之 業 務
considered the business operation of the Group at the material time, it is believed    營 運，本 公 司 認 為 董 事 會 由 經 驗 豐 富 之 專 業
that the Board, which consists of experienced professionals, can function              人士組成，整體可有效運作，而執行董事連同
effectively as a whole, while the executive Directors along with other members         本公司其他高級管理人員則有能力監督本集
of senior management of the Company are capable of overseeing the day-to-day           團在現有穩固企業管治架構下之日常管理工作。
management of the Group under the strong corporate governance structure in
place.

Nevertheless, the Company understands the importance of compliance with the            儘 管 如 此，本 公 司 明 白 遵 守 企 業 管 治 守 則 之
code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code and will continue to review the structure          守則條文第C.2.1條十分重要，並將繼續不時檢
from time to time and consider appointing a chairman/chairlady of the Board as         討有關架構，亦考慮在適當時委任董事會主席。
and when appropriate.

The Group commits to continuously improving its corporate governance practices         本集團承諾透過定期檢討持續改善其企業管
by periodic review to ensure that the Group continues to meet the requirements         治常規以確保本集團繼續達到企業管治守則
of the CG Code.                                                                        之要求。

MODEL CODE FOR SECURITIES TRANSACTIONS BY                                            董事進行證券交易之標準守則

contributions by selected participants and to provide them with incentives in          與 者 作 出 之 貢 獻，並 為 彼 等 提 供 獎 勵 以 留 住

the continual operation and future development and expansion of the Group and          努力，並使參與者與本公司股東利益相符，從
aligning the interests of the participants and the shareholders of the Company.        而實現提升本集團價值此目標。

Impairment assessment of non-current assets of, and interest in, an associate located

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether the consolidated      我 們 的 目 標，是 對 綜 合 財 務 報 表 整 體 是 否 不

to fraud or error, and to issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We     取 得 合 理 保 證，並 出 具 包 括 我 們 意 見 的 核 數
report our opinion solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do          師 報 告。我 們 僅 向 閣 下（作 為 整 體）出 具 本
not assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person for the     報告，除此之外別無其他目的。我們並不就本
contents of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is    報告的內容對任何其他人士承擔任何責任或
not a guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs will always          接受任何義務。合理保證是高水準的保證，但
detect a material misstatement when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud     不 能 保 證 按 照 香 港 審 計 準 則 進 行 的 審 計，在
or error and are considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they       某 一 重 大 錯 誤 陳 述 存 在 時 總 能 發 現。錯 誤 陳
could reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken       述 可 以 由 欺 詐 或 錯 誤 引 起，如 果 合 理 預 期 它
on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.                              們單獨或匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使

1.     GENERAL INFORMATION                                                              1.   一般資料

       Hao Tian International Construction Investment Group Limited (the                     昊天國際建設投資集團有限公司（「本公
       “Company”, together with its subsidiaries, the “Group”) is an exempted                司」，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）在
       limited liability company incorporated in the Cayman Islands. In the                  開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限公司。
       opinion of the directors of the Company, the immediate and ultimate                   本公司董事認為，其直接及最終控股公
       holding company of the Company is Hao Tian Management (China)                         司 為 昊 天 實 業 管 理（中 國）有 限 公 司 及
       Limited and Asia Link Capital Investment Holdings Limited, which are                  亞聯創富控股有限公司，該等公司分別
       incorporated in Hong Kong and the British Virgin Islands respectively, and            於香港及英屬處女群島註冊成立。本公
       the intermediate holding company of the Company is Aceso Life Science                 司之中間控股公司為信銘生命科技集團
       Group Limited with its shares listed on the Main Board of The Stock                   有限公司，其股份於香港聯合交易所有

              Income tax expense represents the sum of current and deferred                            所得稅開支指即期及遞延所得稅開
              income tax expense.                                                                      支的總和。

              The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.                        即期應付稅項是根據本年度之應課
              Taxable profit differs from (loss)/profit before tax because of                           稅溢利計算。應課稅溢利與除稅前
              income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other years and                      （虧損）╱溢利存在差異是由於應課
              items that are never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for                     稅溢利不包括其他年度之應課稅收

              substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.                                 遠毋須課稅及不可扣稅項目。本集

                                                                                                        佈或實質頒佈的稅率計算。

              computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally                    確認。一般情況下，所有因應課稅
              recognised for all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax assets                    暫時差額而產生之遞延稅項負債均

              against which those deductible temporary differences can be                              可能有應課稅溢利可使用該等可扣
              utilised. Such deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised                    稅者時差額予以抵扣為限。如暫時
              if the temporary difference arises from the initial recognition (other                   差 額 是 因 某 交 易 初 次 確 認（業 務 合
              than in a business combination) of assets and liabilities in a                           併 除 外）其 他 資 產 及 負 債 而 產 生，
              transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting                   又不影響應課稅溢利及會計溢利，
              profit and at the time of the transaction does not give rise to equal                    且交易時並不同時產生等額的應課
              taxable and deductible temporary differences. In addition, deferred                      稅暫時差額及可扣減之暫時差額，

                     assessment of the probability of default and loss given                               數。違約概率及違約虧損之評
                     default is based on historical data and forward-looking                               估乃基於過往數據按前瞻性資

                     probability-weighted amount that is determined with the                               計反映無偏頗及概率加權金

              received.                                                                              得款項確認有抵押借款。

              loss.

              On derecognition of an investment in equity instrument which the                       終止確認本集團已選擇在首次確認
              Group has elected on initial recognition to measure at FVTOCI,                         時按以公平值計量且其變化計入其

       differ from these estimates.

       basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised in the period in                檢 討。倘 修 訂 僅 影 響 該 段 期 間，則 會 計
       which the estimate is revised if the revision affects only that period, or in           估計之修訂會於估計被修訂之期間確認，
       the period of the revision and future periods if the revision affects both              或倘修訂同時影響當前期間及日後期間，
       current and future periods.                                                             則修訂會於修訂期間及日後期間確認。

       Ltd (“CESIZ”), owns a land in a special economic zone approved by                     聯 營 公 司CESIZ (Cambodia) Co., Ltd（「 CESIZ」）
       the Council of Ministers (the “Special Economic Zone”) with a size of                 在柬埔寨國公省擁有一塊經部長理事會
       17,252,519 square meters at Koh Kong Province, Cambodia (the “Project                 批准的經濟特區（「經濟特區」），大小為
       Land”). In view the Project Land is located in a developing country which             17,252,519平 方 米 的 土 地（「項 目 土 地」）。
       may be affected by the global economic challenges, the Group performed                鑒於項目土地位於發展中國家，可能受
       impairment assessment on the Project Land and the Group’s interest in                 到全球經濟挑戰的影響，本集團對項目
       this associate. Determining whether impairment loss should be recognised              土地及本集團於該聯營公司之權益進行
       requires an estimation of the recoverable amount of the relevant associate            了減值評估。釐定是否應確認減值虧損
       which is the higher of value in use and fair value less costs of disposal.            要求估計相關聯營公司的可收回金額，
                                                                                             其為使用價值及公平值減出售成本兩者
                                                                                             中之較高者。

       In assessing the recoverable amount of the Group’s interest in this                   於評估本集團於該聯營公司之權益的可
       associate, the management considered the net realizable value on the                  收回金額時，管理層將項目土地的可變
       Project Land as the source of proceeds from the ultimate disposal of the              現淨值視為最終出售該投資所得款項的
       investment.                                                                           來源。

       The information about the impairment assessment of the associate is                   有關該聯營公司減值評估的資料於附註
       disclosed in note 17.                                                                 17披露。

       Trade receivables                                                                       貿易應收款項

       In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly since                               於評估自初始確認後信貸風險是否顯著增
       initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of default occurring                                加時，本集團將於報告日期就金融工具發
       on the financial instrument at the reporting date with the risk perceived                            生的違約風險與初始確認日期感知到的風
       at the date of initial recognition. In making this assessment, the loan                              險進行比較。進行此評估時，本集團管理
       receivables from borrowers are assessed individually by the management                               層乃基於各借款人的財務背景、財務狀況
       of the Group, based on the financial background, financial condition and                             及過往結付記錄（包括逾期日期及違約概
       historical settlement records, including past due dates and probability of                           率）
                                                                                                             ，以及在不付出過多成本或努力的情
       default, of each borrower and reasonable and supportable forward-looking                             況下可獲得的合理且可提供支持依據的前
       information that is available without undue cost or effort. Each borrower                            瞻性資料，對應收借款人的貸款分別進行
       is assigned a risk grading under internal credit ratings to calculate the                            評估。根據內部信用評級對各借款人進行
       ECL, taking into consideration the estimates of expected cash shortfalls                             風險分級，以計算預期信貸虧損，當中計
       which are driven by the estimates of probability of default and expected                             及對違約概率及預期違約損失所帶來的預
       loss given default, including taking into account the amount and timing                              期現金短缺之估計，包括計及抵押品取消
       of cash flows that are expected from foreclosure on the collateral (if any)                          贖回權（如有）預期產生的現金流之金額
       less the costs of selling the collateral. At every reporting date, the financial                     及時間減去出售抵押品的成本。於每個報
       background, financial condition and historical settlement records of each                            告日期，會對各借款人的財務背景、財務
       borrower are reassessed and changes in the forward-looking information                               狀況及過往結付記錄進行重新評估，並考
       are considered.                                                                                      慮前瞻性資料的變化。

35. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS (CONTINUED)                                                 35. 公平值計量（續）

       Some of the Group’s financial instruments are measured at fair values for          就財務報告用途，本集團部分金融工具
       financial reporting purposes.                                                      按公平值計量。

       In estimating the fair values, the Group uses market-observable data to the        於估計公平值時，本集團會使用可取得
       extent it is available. For instruments with significant unobservable inputs       的市場可觀察數據。針對含有第3級重大
       under Level 3, the Group engages third party qualified valuers to perform          不可觀察輸入數據之工具，本集團委聘
       the valuation.                                                                     第三方合資格估值師進行估值。

       Some of the Group’s financial assets and financial liabilities are measured        本集團部分金融資產及金融負債於各報
       at fair values at the end of each reporting period.                                告期末按公平值計量。

       The directors of the Company consider that the carrying amounts of                 本公司董事認為，於綜合財務報表按攤
       financial assets and financial liabilities at amortised cost recognised in the     銷成本確認之金融資產及金融負債之賬
       consolidated financial statements approximate to their fair values.                面值與彼等之公平值相若。
